# de-self-employed-income-tax: figure and rule ledger

Guide: [de-self-employed-income-tax](../../skills/germany/de-self-employed-income-tax.md). Accepted Guide SHA-256: `2280d3e51b479079e099ff054b5d9dc83c8d390fb3b63e86a4db5ea124a74ebe`.
Repackaged from the completed [independent country review](https://github.com/openaccountants/openaccountants/blob/bceaf70083a8c8573ba8558a339ab11b1b4095bf/ledgers/germany-2026-evidence/independent-review/report.md) at `bceaf70083a8c8573ba8558a339ab11b1b4095bf`. The Guide bytes, all current ledger claims and all original-topic destinations are preserved. This is a packaging change; it does not claim a new legal review or accountant attestation.
Original published ledger SHA-256: `268d21a62c0c9cf9775471f683d835eec408b8d9113bb083adbebea1de2afc04`. Full source captures, earlier versions, scripts and review chains remain in the original archive; none is added by this replacement. Source excerpts below retain their actual read dates. Short excerpts identify the previously reviewed operative unit; they do not replace its complete conditions.

| ID | Section / native LF | Exact current wording | Sources / classification |

| 001 | Germany: income tax for the self-employed, from bank statement to tax, tax year 2026; LF19–19 | This skill takes one self-employed person's business transactions for 2026, sorts them into the cash-basis profit statement (Einnahmen-Überschuss-Rechnung, EÜR), and works out the income tax, solidarity surcharge and church tax. It is for the 2026 return, filed in 2027. | Routing / presentation / express reservation / protected attribution, no new positive legal rule.. Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |

| 010 | Who this applies to; LF33–33 | - **In scope.** An individual with a residence or habitual abode in Germany, taxed on worldwide income (§ 1(1) EStG), who earns profit from self-employed work (§ 18 EStG, Anlage S) or a trade (§ 15 EStG, Anlage G), and works out profit by the cash method (§ 4(3) EStG). Single or jointly assessed with a spouse. | [de-self-employed-income-tax.RES](#q-2321781475dec7), [de-self-employed-income-tax.PROF](#q-ae4a1bd565f67f), [de-self-employed-income-tax.TRADE](#q-04699c403d6879), [de-self-employed-income-tax.CASH](#q-52854f74603990), [de-self-employed-income-tax.SPLIT](#q-75b53898a9112f). Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |
| 011 | Who this applies to; LF34–34 | - **Freiberufler or trade.** Only a trade pays trade tax and gets the § 35 credit. The named professions in § 18(1) no. 1 EStG (doctors, lawyers, engineers, architects, tax advisers, journalists, translators and similar) and scientific, artistic, writing and teaching work are Freiberufler. Everything else run for profit is a trade (§ 15(2) EStG). Unclear cases: refer (see de-einkommensteuer-freelancer). | [de-self-employed-income-tax.PROF](#q-ae4a1bd565f67f), [de-self-employed-income-tax.TRADE](#q-04699c403d6879), [de-self-employed-income-tax.CREDIT](#q-90acf60239a4ff). Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |
| 012 | Who this applies to; LF35–35 | - **Cash method or books.** A trader must keep books once the tax office gives notice after turnover over EUR 800,000 or trade profit over EUR 80,000 in a year (§ 141 AO). A Freiberufler can always use the cash method. Books are out of scope here. | [de-self-employed-income-tax.BOOKS](#q-9cfc42fe128e5e), [de-self-employed-income-tax.CASH](#q-52854f74603990). Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |
| 013 | Who this applies to; LF36–36 | - **Who must file.** Without wages, anyone whose total income is above the basic allowance (twice it for spouses) (§ 56 EStDV). With business income the return and the EÜR must be sent electronically (§ 25(4) EStG, § 60(4) EStDV). | [de-self-employed-income-tax.FILEDUTY](#q-e57db73c5443f5), [de-self-employed-income-tax.EFILE](#q-b464024f6933f9), [de-self-employed-income-tax.EEUR](#q-05b6d620d859b8). Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |
| 014 | Who this applies to; LF37–37 | - **Out of scope.** Companies (GmbH, UG): de-corporate-tax. Partnerships (GbR, OHG, KG, PartG), whose profit is fixed at partnership level. Non-residents with German business income: de-non-resident-tax. Selling or closing the business, extraordinary income (§ 34 EStG), retained profits (§ 34a EStG), foreign income and treaties. | Routing / presentation / express reservation / protected attribution, no new positive legal rule.. Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |

| 062 | The taxable base; LF94–94 | \| Transfers between own accounts, loan principal, private withdrawals \| Leave out of ordinary cash receipts/expenses, but record withdrawals and deposits separately. Business-loan interest is subject to § 4(4a) EStG, also under EÜR: overwithdrawals are withdrawals exceeding profit plus deposits. The statutory add-back is 6% of current plus prior overwithdrawals less prior underwithdrawals, capped at the year's interest less EUR 2,050. Profit for this test excludes the add-back itself. Interest on fixed-asset acquisition/production loans is unaffected. The complete multi-year balance and mixed-debt allocation method is not set out here \| | [de-self-employed-income-tax.CASH](#q-52854f74603990), [de-self-employed-income-tax.BUSCOST](#q-7236c6d78e51e4), [de-self-employed-income-tax.INTEREST](#q-5c65a1f8ce414a). Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |

| 064 | The taxable base; LF96–96 | \| Cash withdrawals \| Ask what the cash paid for. Default: leave out \| | Routing / presentation / express reservation / protected attribution, no new positive legal rule.. Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |
| 065 | The taxable base; LF98–102 | **Cars.** A car used more than 50% for business can be a business asset: all costs are expenses and private use is added back at 1% of the gross list price per month (§ 6(1) no. 4 EStG), or by logbook. A private car used for business trips: EUR 0.30 per km or the actual cost share. Home-to-premises travel uses the commuting allowance and, for a business car, the additional adjustment below. Electric and hybrid cars have special rules: refer.<br><br>**Business-car commuting adjustment, ordinary combustion car.** In addition to the separate private-use add-back, § 4(5) no. 6 EStG disallows `max(0, D − A)` for travel between home and the business premises. Under the list-price method, `D = 0.0003 × domestic list price at first registration × full one-way kilometres × calendar months`. `A` is the statutory commuting allowance: for this ordinary car case, `EUR 0.38 × full one-way kilometres × actual commuting days`, applying the § 9(1) no. 4 distance and benefit-reduction conditions. When private use is valued under § 6(1) no. 4 sentence 1 or 3, including the logbook method, use the actual documented costs of these commuting trips as `D` instead. Electric/hybrid reductions and family-home trips need their separate rules.<br><br>For a stipulated ordinary car with a EUR 40,000 list price, 20 full km, 12 months and 200 commuting days, `D = 2,880`, `A = 1,520`, and the extra nondeductible amount is EUR 1,360. With actual/logbook commuting costs of EUR 1,000 the extra amount is zero; with EUR 2,000 it is EUR 480. These amounts are separate from the private-use add-back. | [de-self-employed-income-tax.CAR1](#q-9426328729289f), [de-self-employed-income-tax.CARLOG](#q-bc423f32458f82), [de-self-employed-income-tax.TRAVEL](#q-81cf911cbc1825), [de-self-employed-income-tax.COMMUTE](#q-8e25c3a9c6abf6), [de-self-employed-income-tax.COMMUTEBRIDGE](#q-44db0c5bd0b8c8), [R01.business-car](#q-835dd854e16bb4), [R01.allowance](#q-2caa7a75dc3c37). Bounded DE-R01–08 wording/evidence successor |

| 068 | The taxable base; LF108–108 | **Step 2: from profit to taxable income** (§ 2 EStG). Profit plus any other income = total income. Less extraordinary burdens and child allowances (see de-employee-income-tax). | 4aac878b58b4a12ba61221dd88be2f79d95e04c2 LF31 | - **In scope.** An individual with a residence or habitual abode in Germany, taxed on worldwide income (§ 1(1) EStG), who earns profit from self-employed work (§ 18 EStG, Anlage S) or a trade (§ 15 EStG, Anlage G), and works out profit by the cash method (§ 4(3) EStG). Single or jointly assessed with a spouse. | Exact wording retained at current LF33, Who this applies to  |
| 4aac878b58b4a12ba61221dd88be2f79d95e04c2 LF32 | - **Freiberufler or trade.** Only a trade pays trade tax and gets the § 35 credit. The named professions in § 18(1) no. 1 EStG (doctors, lawyers, engineers, architects, tax advisers, journalists, translators and similar) and scientific, artistic, writing and teaching work are Freiberufler. Everything else run for profit is a trade (§ 15(2) EStG). Unclear cases: refer (see de-einkommensteuer-freelancer). | Exact wording retained at current LF34, Who this applies to  |
| 4aac878b58b4a12ba61221dd88be2f79d95e04c2 LF33 | - **Cash method or books.** A trader must keep books once the tax office gives notice after turnover over EUR 800,000 or trade profit over EUR 80,000 in a year (§ 141 AO). A Freiberufler can always use the cash method. Books are out of scope here. | Exact wording retained at current LF35, Who this applies to  |
| 4aac878b58b4a12ba61221dd88be2f79d95e04c2 LF34 | - **Who must file.** Without wages, anyone whose total income is above the basic allowance (twice it for spouses) (§ 56 EStDV). With business income the return and the EÜR must be sent electronically (§ 25(4) EStG, § 60(4) EStDV). | Exact wording retained at current LF36, Who this applies to  |
| 4aac878b58b4a12ba61221dd88be2f79d95e04c2 LF35 | - **Out of scope.** Companies (GmbH, UG): de-corporate-tax. Partnerships (GbR, OHG, KG, PartG), whose profit is fixed at partnership level. Non-residents with German business income: de-non-resident-tax. Selling or closing the business, extraordinary income (§ 34 EStG), retained profits (§ 34a EStG), foreign income and treaties. | Exact wording retained at current LF37, Who this applies to DE-R06: statutory tax/Soli withdrawal priority/exclusion and qualifying outside-liability exception preserved in summary; detailed-regime referral retained. |

Default: leave out \| | Exact wording retained at current LF96, The taxable base DE-R06: statutory tax/Soli withdrawal priority/exclusion and qualifying outside-liability exception preserved in summary; detailed-regime referral retained. |
| 4aac878b58b4a12ba61221dd88be2f79d95e04c2 LF96 | **Cars.** A car used more than 50% for business can be a business asset: all costs are expenses and private use is added back at 1% of the gross list price per month (§ 6(1) no. 4 EStG), or by logbook. A private car used for business trips: EUR 0.30 per km or the actual cost share. Trips from home to the own office count only at EUR 0.38 per km one way. Electric and hybrid cars have special rules: refer. | Exact wording retained at current LF98, The taxable base DE-R01: business-car commuting positive-difference and actual/logbook method supplied in current Cars/Commuting section; separate private-use method retained. |
| 4aac878b58b4a12ba61221dd88be2f79d95e04c2 LF98 | **Phone and internet.** Deduct the business share shown by records. The employee flat rate from the wage tax guidelines is not a rule for the self-employed. | Exact wording retained at current LF104, The taxable base  |
| 4aac878b58b4a12ba61221dd88be2f79d95e04c2 LF100 | **Mixed and unknown items.** Default to no deduction until the client confirms the business share. | Exact wording retained at current LF106, The taxable base  |
| 4aac878b58b4a12ba61221dd88be2f79d95e04c2 LF102 | **Step 2: from profit to taxable income** (§ 2 EStG). Profit plus any other income = total income. Less special expenses: pension contributions within the cap; basic health and care cover in full; other insurance only if the cap has room; church tax paid, donations, or the EUR 36 lump sum. Less extraordinary burdens and child allowances (see de-employee-income-tax). Round down to whole euros. | Exact wording retained at current LF108, The taxable base  |
| 4aac878b58b4a12ba61221dd88be2f79d95e04c2 LF104 | **Step 3: tax.** Apply the tariff (or splitting). For a trader, subtract the § 35 credit: the lowest of 4.0 × Messbetrag, the tax that falls on trade income (positive trade income / all positive income × tariff tax), and the trade tax actually payable. Unused credit is lost. Then the surcharge on the tax after the credit, and church tax on the tax before the credit (§ 51a(2) EStG). Subtract prepayments paid. | Exact wording retained at current LF110, The taxable base  |
| 4aac878b58b4a12ba61221dd88be2f79d95e04c2 LF106 | **Losses.** A loss offsets other income of the same year, then goes back one year or forward without time limit (§ 10d EStG; see de-einkommensteuer-freelancer). | DE-L06 source correction at current LF112; actual10d(1) includes second preceding year under statutory cap, not the historical one-year limitation.  |
| 4aac878b58b4a12ba61221dd88be2f79d95e04c2 LF110 | \| Step \| Rule \| | Exact wording retained at current LF116, Deadlines and filing  |
| 4aac878b58b4a12ba61221dd88be2f79d95e04c2 LF112 | \| Register the business \| Within one month of starting (§ 138 AO): a trade with the municipality, a Freiberufler with the tax office online \| | Exact wording retained at current LF118, Deadlines and filing  |
| 4aac878b58b4a12ba61221dd88be2f79d95e04c2 LF113 | \| Forms \| Main form, Anlage S or G, Anlage EÜR with Anlage AVEÜR (one set per business), Anlage Vorsorgeaufwand, Anlage Sonderausgaben. Electronic, through ELSTER (§ 25(4) EStG, § 60(4) EStDV) \| | Exact wording retained at current LF119, Deadlines and filing  |
| 4aac878b58b4a12ba61221dd88be2f79d95e04c2 LF114 | \| Own return \| Seven months after year end (§ 149(2) AO): 31 July 2027 is a Saturday, so Monday 2 August 2027 (§ 108(3) AO) \| | Exact wording retained at current LF120, Deadlines and filing  |
| 4aac878b58b4a12ba61221dd88be2f79d95e04c2 LF115 | \| Prepared by a tax adviser \| Last day of February of the second year after (§ 149(3) AO): Tuesday 29 February 2028. The office can call for an earlier return with four months' notice (§ 149(4) AO) \| | Exact wording retained at current LF121, Deadlines and filing  |
| 4aac878b58b4a12ba61221dd88be2f79d95e04c2 LF116 | \| Balance due \| One month after the notice (§ 36(4) EStG) \| | Exact wording retained at current LF122, Deadlines and filing  |
| 4aac878b58b4a12ba61221dd88be2f79d95e04c2 LF117 | \| Prepayments \| 10 March, 10 June, 10 September, 10 December, set by notice from the last assessed tax. The office can adjust them until 15 months after year end; a rise after year end is due one month after the notice and only if at least EUR 5,000 (§ 37(3) to (5) EStG) \| | Exact wording retained at current LF123, Deadlines and filing  |
| 4aac878b58b4a12ba61221dd88be2f79d95e04c2 LF121 | - **Late-filing surcharge (§ 152 AO).** May be set for any late return. Must be set if the return is not in within 14 months after year end (for 2026: after 29 February 2028), unless the deadline was extended, the tax is nil, or the tax is not more than prepayments and tax withheld. Amount: 0.25% per started month of the tax less prepayments and tax withheld, at least EUR 25 per started month, at most EUR 25,000, rounded down to euros (§ 152(5) and (10) AO). | Exact wording retained at current LF127, Penalties and interest  |
| 4aac878b58b4a12ba61221dd88be2f79d95e04c2 LF122 | - **Interest (§ 233a AO).** From 15 months after year end (1 April 2028 for 2026) until the notice: 0.15% per full month, 1.8% a year (§ 238(1a) AO), on the balance rounded down to EUR 50. Refunds earn the same. | Exact wording retained at current LF128, Penalties and interest  |
| 4aac878b58b4a12ba61221dd88be2f79d95e04c2 LF123 | - **Late payment (§ 240 AO).** 1% per started month on the overdue amount rounded down to EUR 50. Applies to late prepayments too. | Exact wording retained at current LF129, Penalties and interest DE-R01: business-car commuting positive-difference and actual/logbook method supplied in current Cars/Commuting section; separate private-use method retained. |
| 4aac878b58b4a12ba61221dd88be2f79d95e04c2 LF124 | - **Wrong returns.** Tax evasion is a crime (§ 370 AO). Reckless understatement is an administrative offence with a fine of up to EUR 50,000 (§ 378 AO). Either case: refer. | Exact wording retained at current LF130, Penalties and interest  |
| 4aac878b58b4a12ba61221dd88be2f79d95e04c2 LF128 | - **Non-residents.** A person without a German home or habitual abode is taxed only on German income, including profit from a German permanent establishment or self-employed work done in Germany (§ 49 EStG). Use de-non-resident-tax. | Exact wording retained at current LF134, Cross-border points  |
| 4aac878b58b4a12ba61221dd88be2f79d95e04c2 LF129 | - **Foreign clients.** Fees from foreign clients for work done in Germany by a resident are German income in full. VAT on cross-border services: germany-vat-return. | Exact wording retained at current LF135, Cross-border points  |
| 4aac878b58b4a12ba61221dd88be2f79d95e04c2 LF130 | - **Foreign tax, foreign establishment, moving in or out.** Foreign tax credits are in de-non-resident-tax (§ 34c EStG). A treaty may exempt profit of a foreign establishment, which then raises the rate (§ 32b EStG): refer. Departure with shares: leaving-germany-exit-tax. | DE-L09 source correction at current LF136; treaty/progression and credit/exit referrals retained with actual32b exceptions.  |
| 4aac878b58b4a12ba61221dd88be2f79d95e04c2 LF131 | - **§ 7g abroad.** The investment deduction needs the asset used in a German establishment (§ 7g(1) EStG). | Exact wording retained at current LF137, Cross-border points  |
| 4aac878b58b4a12ba61221dd88be2f79d95e04c2 LF137 | Anna is a self-employed designer in Hamburg, single, no church, normal VAT scheme. 2026 fees net of VAT: EUR 72,000. | 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF33 | - **In scope.** An individual with a residence or habitual abode in Germany, taxed on worldwide income (§ 1(1) EStG), who earns profit from self-employed work (§ 18 EStG, Anlage S) or a trade (§ 15 EStG, Anlage G), and works out profit by the cash method (§ 4(3) EStG). Single or jointly assessed with a spouse. | Exact wording retained at current LF33, Who this applies to  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF34 | - **Freiberufler or trade.** Only a trade pays trade tax and gets the § 35 credit. The named professions in § 18(1) no. 1 EStG (doctors, lawyers, engineers, architects, tax advisers, journalists, translators and similar) and scientific, artistic, writing and teaching work are Freiberufler. Everything else run for profit is a trade (§ 15(2) EStG). Unclear cases: refer (see de-einkommensteuer-freelancer). | Exact wording retained at current LF34, Who this applies to  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF35 | - **Cash method or books.** A trader must keep books once the tax office gives notice after turnover over EUR 800,000 or trade profit over EUR 80,000 in a year (§ 141 AO). A Freiberufler can always use the cash method. Books are out of scope here. | Exact wording retained at current LF35, Who this applies to  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF36 | - **Who must file.** Without wages, anyone whose total income is above the basic allowance (twice it for spouses) (§ 56 EStDV). With business income the return and the EÜR must be sent electronically (§ 25(4) EStG, § 60(4) EStDV). | Exact wording retained at current LF36, Who this applies to  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF37 | - **Out of scope.** Companies (GmbH, UG): de-corporate-tax. Partnerships (GbR, OHG, KG, PartG), whose profit is fixed at partnership level. Non-residents with German business income: de-non-resident-tax. Selling or closing the business, extraordinary income (§ 34 EStG), retained profits (§ 34a EStG), foreign income and treaties. | Exact wording retained at current LF37, Who this applies to DE-R06: statutory tax/Soli withdrawal priority/exclusion and qualifying outside-liability exception preserved in summary; detailed-regime referral retained. |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF79 | **Step 1: profit (Anlage EÜR).** Receipts when received, costs when paid (§ 11 EStG). Regular payments made shortly before or after the year end count in the year they belong to. | Exact wording retained at current LF79, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF81 | \| On the bank statement \| Treatment \| | Exact wording retained at current LF81, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF83 | \| Client payments, fees (Honorar), platform payouts (Stripe, PayPal) \| Receipts. Normal VAT scheme: the VAT collected is also a receipt and VAT paid to the tax office an expense. Kleinunternehmer: the gross amount \| | Exact wording retained at current LF83, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF84 | \| Rent for business premises, office supplies, software, advertising, bank fees, adviser fees, chamber dues, business liability insurance \| Expense in full \| | Exact wording retained at current LF84, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF85 | \| Restaurant with a client (Bewirtung) \| 70% of the reasonable cost. Needs a receipt naming place, date, guests and reason, with the till receipt. Input VAT is still fully deductible \| | Exact wording retained at current LF85, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF86 | \| Gifts to clients \| In full if the year's gifts to that person cost EUR 50 or less; above that, nothing at all. Record each recipient \| | Exact wording retained at current LF86, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF87 | \| Rent of the home, home office \| Day rate EUR 6 per day worked mainly at home, max EUR 1,260. Room costs only if the room is the centre of all work \| | Exact wording retained at current LF87, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF88 | \| Fuel, car costs \| Depends on the car method; see below. No method confirmed: deduct nothing yet \| | Exact wording retained at current LF88, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF89 | \| Laptop, phone, furniture \| Over EUR 800 net: depreciate (computers and software: one year allowed). EUR 800 or less: expense now or pool \| | Exact wording retained at current LF89, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF90 | \| Health, care, pension insurance (Krankenkasse, DRV, KSK) \| Not a business expense. Special expense in Step 2 \| | Exact wording retained at current LF90, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF91 | \| Income tax, surcharge, church tax, prepayments \| Never a business expense (§ 12 no. 3 EStG). Church tax paid is a special expense in Step 2 (§ 10(1) no. 4 EStG) \| | Exact wording retained at current LF91, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF92 | \| Trade tax (Gewerbesteuer) \| Never deductible (§ 4(5b) EStG). Records that call it an expense are wrong for any year since 2008 \| | Exact wording retained at current LF92, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF93 | \| Fines from a court or authority \| Not deductible (§ 4(5) no. 8 EStG) \| | Exact wording retained at current LF93, The taxable base  |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF95 | \| Wages, rent received, interest, dividends \| Not business income. Other forms (Anlage N, V, KAP) \| | Exact wording retained at current LF95, The taxable base  |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF98 | **Cars.** A car used more than 50% for business can be a business asset: all costs are expenses and private use is added back at 1% of the gross list price per month (§ 6(1) no. 4 EStG), or by logbook. A private car used for business trips: EUR 0.30 per km or the actual cost share. Trips from home to the own office count only at EUR 0.38 per km one way. Electric and hybrid cars have special rules: refer. | Exact wording retained at current LF98, The taxable base DE-R01: business-car commuting positive-difference and actual/logbook method supplied in current Cars/Commuting section; separate private-use method retained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF100 | **Phone and internet.** Deduct the business share shown by records. The employee flat rate from the wage tax guidelines is not a rule for the self-employed. | Exact wording retained at current LF104, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF102 | **Mixed and unknown items.** Default to no deduction until the client confirms the business share. | Exact wording retained at current LF106, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF104 | **Step 2: from profit to taxable income** (§ 2 EStG). Profit plus any other income = total income. Less special expenses: pension contributions within the cap; basic health and care cover in full; other insurance only if the cap has room; church tax paid, donations, or the EUR 36 lump sum. Less extraordinary burdens and child allowances (see de-employee-income-tax). Round down to whole euros. | Exact wording retained at current LF108, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF106 | **Step 3: tax.** Apply the tariff (or splitting). For a trader, subtract the § 35 credit: the lowest of 4.0 × Messbetrag, the tax that falls on trade income (positive trade income / all positive income × tariff tax), and the trade tax actually payable. Unused credit is lost. Then the surcharge on the tax after the credit, and church tax on the tax before the credit (§ 51a(2) EStG). Subtract prepayments paid. | Exact wording retained at current LF110, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF108 | **Losses.** A loss offsets other income of the same year, then goes back one year or forward without time limit (§ 10d EStG; see de-einkommensteuer-freelancer). | DE-L06 source correction at current LF112; actual10d(1) includes second preceding year under statutory cap, not the historical one-year limitation.  |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF115 | \| Forms \| Main form, Anlage S or G, Anlage EÜR with Anlage AVEÜR (one set per business), Anlage Vorsorgeaufwand, Anlage Sonderausgaben. The office can adjust them until 15 months after year end; a rise after year end is due one month after the notice and only if at least EUR 5,000 (§ 37(3) to (5) EStG) \| | Exact wording retained at current LF123, Deadlines and filing  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF123 | - **Late-filing surcharge (§ 152 AO).** May be set for any late return. Must be set if the return is not in within 14 months after year end (for 2026: after 29 February 2028), unless the deadline was extended, the tax is nil, or the tax is not more than prepayments and tax withheld. Amount: 0.25% per started month of the tax less prepayments and tax withheld, at least EUR 25 per started month, at most EUR 25,000, rounded down to euros (§ 152(5) and (10) AO). | Exact wording retained at current LF127, Penalties and interest  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF124 | - **Interest (§ 233a AO).** From 15 months after year end (1 April 2028 for 2026) until the notice: 0.15% per full month, 1.8% a year (§ 238(1a) AO), on the balance rounded down to EUR 50. Refunds earn the same. | Exact wording retained at current LF128, Penalties and interest  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF125 | - **Late payment (§ 240 AO).** 1% per started month on the overdue amount rounded down to EUR 50. Applies to late prepayments too. | Exact wording retained at current LF129, Penalties and interest DE-R01: business-car commuting positive-difference and actual/logbook method supplied in current Cars/Commuting section; separate private-use method retained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF126 | - **Wrong returns.** Tax evasion is a crime (§ 370 AO). Reckless understatement is an administrative offence with a fine of up to EUR 50,000 (§ 378 AO). Either case: refer. | Exact wording retained at current LF130, Penalties and interest  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF130 | - **Non-residents.** A person without a German home or habitual abode is taxed only on German income, including profit from a German permanent establishment or self-employed work done in Germany (§ 49 EStG). Use de-non-resident-tax. | Exact wording retained at current LF134, Cross-border points  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF131 | - **Foreign clients.** Fees from foreign clients for work done in Germany by a resident are German income in full. VAT on cross-border services: germany-vat-return. | Exact wording retained at current LF135, Cross-border points  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF132 | - **Foreign tax, foreign establishment, moving in or out.** Foreign tax credits are in de-non-resident-tax (§ 34c EStG). A treaty may exempt profit of a foreign establishment, which then raises the rate (§ 32b EStG): refer. Departure with shares: leaving-germany-exit-tax. | DE-L09 source correction at current LF136; treaty/progression and credit/exit referrals retained with actual32b exceptions.  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF133 | - **§ 7g abroad.** The investment deduction needs the asset used in a German establishment (§ 7g(1) EStG). | Exact wording retained at current LF137, Cross-border points  |
